Extended `test_mob_spawn_positions.gd` to cover cap behavior, hidden-cell selection, serialization, and the actor cooldown path. Full local headless suite green (272 checks, Godot 4.7.1).

- Tags: mobs, level-generation, source-fidelity, audit-S07
- Fixed regular-level mob spawn placement under-filling floors. `RegularLevel.mob_spawn_positions()` used to consume one requested mob even when a room placement failed, and the no-standard-room fallback returned a single position instead of filling the requested count. Placement now counts successful cells, falls back without duplicates when room attempts stall, and has headless coverage for normal rooms, failed room placement, and fallback-only levels. Full local headless suite green (263 checks, Godot 4.7.1).

- Tags: wands, combat, source-fidelity, audit-S14
- Routed Wand of Disintegration damage through `Char.take_damage()` instead of mutating HP directly. Disintegration still bypasses physical armor because `take_damage()` receives already-raw magical damage, but it now respects shielding, Arcane Armor, wake-on-damage hooks, and the shared death path like the other wand subclasses. Added `tests/cases/test_wand_of_disintegration.gd` to cover Barrier absorption, Arcane Armor reduction, and exactly-once death handling.

- Tags: mobile, camera, zoom, web, playtesting
- Fixed mobile web runs starting heavily zoomed in after the browser-viewport scaling fixes. `GameCamera` had forced mobile web to start at `4.5x`; once the game used the actual phone-sized logical viewport, that showed only a tiny slice of the dungeon. Mobile now starts at `1.5x`, can pinch/setting zoom out to `1x`, and still allows zooming in up to `10x`. Added `tests/cases/test_game_camera_mobile_zoom.gd` to lock the mobile defaults.

- Tags: mobile, hud, toolbar, web, playtesting
- Fixed the in-run mobile HUD using the Godot canvas size when mobile browsers report a larger backing canvas than the visible viewport. `HUD` now mirrors the hero-select fix by laying out against `window.innerWidth/innerHeight` for portrait/small/mobile web contexts and polling browser viewport changes so collapsing address bars or orientation changes relayout the toolbar and status strip. Added portrait safe-area coverage to `test_mobile_hud_input.gd` to keep the toolbar above the bottom inset and the status overlay visible.

- Tags: mobile, hero-select, portrait, web, playtesting
- Fixed the character-select action row falling below the visible panel on tight portrait mobile viewports. `HeroSelectScene` now uses a tighter portrait layout when vertical space is short: smaller splash crop, reduced panel inset/gaps, compressed description/stat blocks, narrower hero buttons, and slimmer Back/Start buttons. Follow-up: mobile Web can report a desktop-sized Godot canvas while the browser viewport is portrait, so `HeroSelectScene` now lays out against `window.innerWidth/innerHeight` when the browser viewport is portrait or small and polls that size during browser chrome changes. Added `tests/cases/test_hero_select_portrait_layout.gd` to lock both the 375px portrait mobile budget and the browser-vs-canvas viewport selection.

- Tags: bosses, hud, combat, fidelity, spd-parity, audit-S27, audit-S35
- Wired the dead boss HP bar signals. `HUD` already subscribed to `boss_fight_started`, `boss_damaged`, and `boss_defeated`, but no gameplay path emitted them, so bosses fought without the original-style top HP bar. `Mob` now recognizes current boss mob IDs (plus the existing `BOSS` property), starts the bar when a boss becomes active or is first damaged, updates it on non-fatal damage, and hides it on defeat. Added `tests/cases/test_boss_hp_signals.gd` to cover non-boss silence, boss start payload, HP update payload, and fatal hide ordering. Full local headless suite green (244 checks, Godot 4.7.1).

- Tags: mobs, swarm, combat, fidelity, spd-parity, audit-S05
- Fixed Swarm of Flies split duplicating HP instead of dividing it (audit S05 P1). `Swarm._split()` set `child.hp = hp` (a full copy) and never reduced the parent, so every split roughly *doubled* total swarm HP and over-granted XP; a swarm chipped below half could balloon in effective HP. SPD's `Swarm.split()` hands half the current HP to the clone and subtracts it from the parent (HP conserved). Fix (`sewer/swarm.gd`): `child_hp = hp / 2`, child gets `child_hp` with `hp_max`/`ht` mirroring the parent cap (previously `ht` was mis-set to raw current HP), then `hp -= child_hp`. Also added an `hp >= 2` guard to the split trigger so a near-dead swarm can't spawn a 0-HP child. Added `tests/cases/test_swarm_split.gd` (division, conservation, non-duplication, cap mirroring, split-once flags) and registered it in the runner; verified it distinguishes the fixed code from the pre-fix duplication. Full local headless suite green (235 checks, Godot 4.7.1). Out-of-scope follow-up: the child is still a fresh `Swarm.new()` with base depth-1 attack/defense/damage rather than the parent's depth-scaled combat stats — separate from the flagged HP regression.

- Tags: levels, traps, halls, fidelity, spd-parity, audit-S09
- Fixed Demon Halls regular-floor trap generation falling back to the base early-game pool (WornDart/Poison/Fire/Alarm/Teleport). `HallsLevel` now has its own weighted late-game trap table based on the original SPD Halls trap weights, using the trap classes currently implemented in the port: Frost, Storm, Corrosion, Blazing, Rockfall, Flashing, Guardian, Disarming, Summoning, Warping, Cursing, Grim, and Pitfall. Added `tests/cases/test_halls_traps.gd` to lock the weighted boundaries and assert Halls never selects the early fallback traps. Full local headless suite green (225 checks, Godot 4.7.1). Remaining parity note: upstream SPD also includes Disintegration, Weakening, Distortion, Gateway, and Geyser in the Halls pool; those trap classes are not present in this port yet.

- Tags: mobile, hud, toolbar, web, playtesting
- Tightened the compact mobile toolbar for narrow portrait viewports. The previous compact sizing still needed about 380px before browser safe-area/panel padding, so a 375px-wide phone viewport could clip or hide controls even after touch routing was fixed. `Toolbar` now receives its available width from `HUD`, switches to a narrower compact layout at <=430px, keeps Bag/Wait/Find/Menu plus the first two quickslots visible, and hides only decorative separators. Added headless coverage to `test_mobile_hud_input.gd` asserting the toolbar fits a 375px portrait budget after HUD panel margins. Full local headless suite green (184 checks, Godot 4.7.1).

- Tags: mobile, hud, controls, web, playtesting
- Fixed mobile HUD taps being followed by Godot's synthesized mouse click. The scene already handled real touch release on HUD controls, but the mouse-suppression gate exempted HUD positions, so mobile web could activate a toolbar/window control and then leak a map click/hero action from the synthetic left-click. `GameScene` now suppresses synthesized touch-mouse events for the whole short post-touch window, including over-HUD positions, while preserving direct mouse/desktop input outside that window. Also routed touch release through the party-row focus buttons, which were previously included in HUD hit-testing but not activated by the custom touch path. Added `tests/cases/test_mobile_hud_input.gd` and registered it in the headless runner. Full local headless suite green (181 checks, Godot 4.7.1).

- Tags: wands, combat, fidelity, spd-parity, audit-S14
- Fixed Wand of Frost always paralysing (audit S14 P1). `WandOfFrost.on_zap` applied `Cripple` (chill) and then checked `has_buff("Cripple")` on the same target - always true because it had just been added - so `Paralysis` (freeze) fired on *every* frost hit instead of only when the target was already chilled. SPD's `Frost.freeze()` only freezes an already-chilled target. Fix: capture `was_chilled` *before* adding the new Cripple, and gate the freeze on that (`wand.gd:430-442`). Added `tests/cases/test_wand_of_frost.gd` (fresh target -> chilled but not frozen; pre-chilled target -> frozen) and registered it in the runner; verified it fails against the pre-fix code and passes after. Full headless suite green (177 checks). NOTE: the S14 wand P0 "wands never recharge" is already fixed/tested (PR #5 `belongings.recharge_wands` per hero turn) - NEXT-STEPS.md is stale on that. Remaining S14 P1s still open in `wand.gd`: Corruption makes an enemy not an ally (string alignment + Amok), Disintegration bypasses `take_damage`, Warding spawns no sentry, use-to-id stalls after 5 zaps.

## 2026-07-16

- Tags: blobs, gas, simulation, fidelity, spd-parity
- Brought the blob/gas layer to life (audit S20 vertical slice). Blobs were dead: `Blob.act()` was never driven, so every seeded gas/fire/web sat frozen at its origin, never spreading, poisoning, or decaying - the root cause of the S15/S19 one-shot-buff workarounds. Changes: (1) `Blob` now exposes `tick()` (spread -> apply effects -> decay -> prune) separate from `act()`, with `_spread()` no longer mutating `active_cells` mid-iteration and a row-wrap guard on cardinal neighbors; added `Blob.deserialize()`. (2) `Level.tick_blobs()` drives all blobs once per hero round (mirrors `tick_pending_bombs`), pruning burned-out blobs; wired into `SceneFeedbackCoordinator.on_round_completed`. (3) `Level.add_blob(blob, cell, amount)` now honors density and merges same-type blobs into one instance (SPD keeps one blob per type per level; prevents duplicate entries double-applying effects). (4) Fixed `fetid_rat.gd` toxic-gas drop, which crashed on `seed()`/`add_blob()` arg-count mismatches. Added `tests/cases/test_blobs.gd` (spread, row-wrap guard, decay/prune lifecycle, ToxicGas poison, serialize round-trip, Level merge + tick_blobs). All 65 headless checks pass.
- NEXT SLICE: blobs still tick per *hero round* rather than per game turn (approximation - fine for hero-paced gas, note if mob-time gas matters). Persistence still round-trips live Node dicts via `store_var` rather than `Blob.serialize()`/factory (`blob.deserialize()` now exists but isn't wired into save_manager). Offensive potions (ToxicGas/ParalyticGas/LiquidFlame/Frost, S15) and gas traps/plants should now be re-pointed at real blobs via `add_blob` instead of one-shot 3x3 buffs. Missing SPD blob classes: Freezing/CausticGas/StenchGas/StormCloud/Regrowth/SmokeScreen (SmokeScreen also unblocks the dead LOS branch in `level.gd:257`).

## 2026-05-09

- Tags: multiplayer, movement, sync
- Added explicit hero/mob movement events for online play. Hosts now broadcast movement events when heroes or mobs step, and non-host clients immediately animate the corresponding sprite movement instead of waiting only for the next authoritative snapshot to reposition actors.

- Tags: architecture, framework, planning
- Added `framework-extraction-roadmap.md` as the concrete planning document for turning this codebase into a reusable 2D turn-based game framework. The note defines target architecture layers, extraction phases, what should remain SPD-specific, and why the current multiplayer hardening work is also framework-seam work rather than a separate initiative.

- Tags: multiplayer, mobs, presentation
- Extended the online world-event lane to mob reveals. Hosts now broadcast explicit reveal events for cases like Mimics, and non-host clients immediately spawn the revealed mob sprite plus `Revealed!` feedback instead of waiting for the next snapshot to make the surprise interaction visible.

- Tags: multiplayer, combat, presentation
- Extended the online world-event lane to explicit combat readability events that snapshots do not convey well on their own. Hosts now broadcast attack misses and mob defeats, and non-host clients immediately show miss feedback and death-burst/audio cues instead of having to infer those moments only from later state correction.

- Tags: combat, buffs, presentation, multiplayer
- Broadened the new status-effect presentation path beyond Poison. `GameScene` now uses a shared feedback table for several high-signal debuffs including Burning, Paralysis, Blindness, Chill, Frozen, Rooted, Cripple, Weakness, and Ooze, so both local and online clients get readable burst/status cues when those effects are applied, and focused heroes also get effect-specific tick feedback when damage comes from a buff source.

- Tags: combat, buffs, multiplayer, presentation
- Added real poison feedback. Buff application now emits a status-effect event, `GameScene` now shows poison burst/status feedback when Poison is applied, poison tick damage on the focused hero now also gets a poison-specific burst/status cue, and the online world-event lane now propagates poison-application feedback to non-host clients.

- Tags: multiplayer, sync, hardening
- Hardened the new online world-event presentation path against duplicate client feedback. `GameScene` now keeps a short suppression window for pickup and door positions after explicit world events land, so the next authoritative snapshot can confirm the same state change without replaying a second `Pickup` or `Open` cue on non-host clients.

- Tags: multiplayer, plants, presentation
- Extended the online world-event lane to planting and plant activation. Hosts now broadcast explicit plant events, and non-host clients immediately update local plant state/terrain, spawn plant visuals, and play the existing planted/activation VFX instead of waiting for a later snapshot to imply that flora changed on the floor.

- Tags: multiplayer, items, presentation
- Extended the online world-event lane to explicit item drops. Hosts now broadcast dropped-item events with serialized item data, and non-host clients immediately update their local floor state, spawn the dropped item sprite with drop animation, and show `Drop` feedback instead of waiting for a later snapshot to imply that a floor mutation happened.

- Tags: multiplayer, traps, presentation
- Extended the online world-event lane to trap triggers. Hosts now broadcast explicit trap-triggered events, and non-host `GameScene` clients react immediately with trap SFX, tile/fog refresh, a `Trap!` popup, and warning text instead of waiting for the next snapshot to imply that a hidden world interaction happened.

- Tags: multiplayer, networking, presentation
- Added a lightweight host-to-client world-event lane on top of the existing snapshot model. `NetworkManager` can now broadcast explicit online world events, and `GameScene` uses that for pickups and door opens so non-host clients see those interactions immediately with item/door feedback instead of waiting only for snapshot diff presentation.

- Tags: multiplayer, sync, presentation
- Continued online snapshot polish by adding diff-based world-action feedback on clients. `GameScene` now captures lightweight level state before same-floor host snapshots, replays visible door-open and secret-door-reveal cues when terrain changes, shows remote `Pickup` feedback when floor heaps disappear under a hero, and surfaces a clearer `Ascended` / `Descended` arrival popup after host-owned floor transitions.

- Tags: multiplayer, minimap, readability
- Continued the roadmap’s co-op readability pass by making maps party-aware. `Minimap` and `WndMap` now render the full party instead of only the focused hero, with allied heroes shown as distinct cyan markers while the focused/local hero remains the white marker. This makes 3-4 player shared-floor runs easier to read at a glance.

- Tags: multiplayer, disconnect, resilience
- Hardened host-loss handling across online scenes. `LobbyScene`, `HeroSelectScene`, `LoadingScene`, and `GameScene` now all react to `Disconnected from host.` by warning the player and returning cleanly to `TitleScene` instead of leaving clients stranded in a dead lobby, setup flow, loading screen, or in-progress run.

- Tags: multiplayer, lobby, session
- Improved lobby/session transition clarity. `NetworkManager` now exposes whether an online run is already active, `LobbyScene` now distinguishes `Lobby open` from `Run in progress`, hides ready controls once a run has started, and prevents the host from trying to start again. `TitleScene` now also shows reconnect-oriented client messaging when joining an already active host session.

- Tags: multiplayer, hud, ux
- Added an explicit online-state banner to `HUD`. During online sessions the HUD now shows whether the local player is on `Your turn`, `Waiting for <player>`, `Waiting for party state...`, or `Spectating another hero`, so co-op state ownership is visible without having to infer it from snapshots or party focus alone.

- Tags: multiplayer, client, ux
- Added lightweight online client-side action preview in `GameScene`. When a non-host player submits a move, attack, throw, zap, interact, or search action, the local hero sprite now previews that intent immediately while the host still remains authoritative for the actual resolved state and later snapshot correction.

- Tags: multiplayer, sync, networking
- Improved online live-sync responsiveness in `GameScene`. Host snapshots are now coalesced through a queued sync path instead of only waiting for clean turn-boundary moments, and that queue is now triggered by input-owner changes, hero movement, item pickup, mob damage, trap activation, seed planting, round completion, and visible mob actions. This should make non-host clients catch up sooner during action-heavy moments without abandoning the existing host-authoritative snapshot model.

- Tags: multiplayer, spectator, hud
- Improved co-op death/spectator UX. `GameManager` now exposes local-owned-hero and spectating helpers, local focus cycling skips dead heroes when living options exist, `StatusPane` shows when the local player is spectating or focused on a down hero, and the party HUD row now marks dead heroes explicitly and stops offering dead-focus buttons as normal active selections.

- Tags: multiplayer, loading, startup
- Fixed a multi-hero startup crash in `LoadingScene`: party placement around the staircase no longer uses raw `DIRS_8` offset math that can wrap across rows and probe invalid cells. The spawn search is now coordinate-safe, which should stop party sizes above 1 from tripping out-of-bounds errors during level entry.

- Tags: multiplayer, hero-select, lobby
- Tightened the online class-loadout flow: `HeroSelectScene` now labels party slots and summaries with the actual connected player names, and host online run start now validates against the current lobby player count before launching. `NetworkManager` also normalizes online `party_classes` to exactly the synchronized player list size during run-start sanitization.

- Tags: multiplayer, reconnect, resync
- Added first-pass online reconnect support. `NetworkManager` now remembers the active online run config plus latest host snapshot, rebinds reconnecting players to their original hero slot by saved multiplayer name, updates host-side hero ownership to the new peer ID, and immediately resends run-start plus snapshot data to late/reconnecting clients. `LobbyScene` now also listens for online run-start while already open, so reconnecting clients can jump straight back into `LoadingScene` for resync instead of getting stranded in the lobby.

- Tags: multiplayer, lobby, identity
- Smoothed the co-op lobby flow further for `v0.1.2`: `NetworkManager` now persists the multiplayer player name, last join code, and preferred port/player-cap across sessions, `TitleScene` preloads those saved values into the host/join UI, and online `HeroSelectScene` now locks party size to the actual connected lobby player count instead of allowing extra local-only party slots.

- Tags: multiplayer, networking, authority
- Continued the online co-op bridge into a real host-authoritative prototype: online run-start config now carries a shared `run_seed`, `LoadingScene` uses that seed during `GameManager.new_game(...)`, and online clients no longer activate heroes/mobs into `TurnManager` when entering the dungeon.
- Added the first host-to-client run snapshot feed: `NetworkManager` now emits `run_snapshot_received`, `GameScene` broadcasts authoritative snapshots from the host when input ownership settles on a hero turn, and online clients now apply those snapshots to refresh level state, hero state, sprites, fog, HUD, and local input availability.
- Split `GameScene` input resolution more cleanly for online play by tracking `_network_input_slot`, so online clients now resolve the acting hero from the host-owned turn slot instead of falling back to whatever hero only happens to be locally focused.
- Extended that authority path through stairs/floor changes: the host now broadcasts an online level-transition notice when the party ascends or descends, clients freeze local input and wait for the next authoritative snapshot, and cross-depth snapshots now rebuild the client from the host's new floor instead of requiring a local deterministic level generation step.
- Continued the online authority cleanup in the UI layer: added `EventBus.request_hero_action(action)` so `HUD` quickslots and `WndItem` no longer call `hero.submit_action(...)` or `item.execute(...)` directly for use/throw/zap flows, and temporary client-side guards now block inventory-only mutations like equip/unequip/quickslot assignment/talent upgrades until those paths have real host-authoritative commands.
- Finished the first host-authoritative inventory command layer in `GameScene`: online action envelopes now encode stable `item_ref` / `target_pos` data instead of live object references, the host resolves those back into concrete items/targets before simulation, and equip/unequip/drop/quickslot/talent-upgrade/feed-seed actions now run through the same host-owned request path instead of mutating directly from the UI.
- Added first-pass co-op death rules: `GameScene` now treats hero death as a party event instead of immediate solo game-over, only transitions to death when all heroes are dead, hands local focus off to a living hero for spectating when possible, and host online runs now broadcast explicit run-end messages so clients follow party wipe / victory cleanly instead of getting stranded after the host finishes.
- Smoothed the online session UX so players do not have to start with raw IP/port entry: `TitleScene` now supports one-click `Quick Host`, join-by-code input, remembers the last join code, and auto-populates host codes; `LobbyScene` now shows the current join code and can copy it to the clipboard; and `NetworkManager` now exposes preferred local address / join-code helpers to make session sharing feel more like a real game flow than a manual network form.
- Reshaped that flow to match the intended model: `Host Co-op` in `TitleScene` now immediately creates a session and drops the player into the lobby, while `Join Co-op` is the explicit path for entering host info. `LobbyScene` now also has a `Settings` button with a small host-settings panel so player name, port, and player-cap changes can be adjusted from inside the lobby instead of forcing hosts through the title-screen network form first.

- Tags: multiplayer, ui, bootstrap
- Continued Phase 1 co-op groundwork by adding a real party bootstrap UI to `HeroSelectScene`: party size can now be set from 1-4, each active party slot can be assigned its own class, `Tab` cycles the edited slot, and Start now passes a `party_classes` payload into `LoadingScene` so local multi-hero runs can be created without debug-only metadata.
- Finished the last obvious focused-vs-input leak in `GameScene` targeting flow so targeted actions now validate range/visibility from the acting hero instead of whichever hero is only being viewed locally.
- Hardened the menu path against parser failures in `HeroSelectScene` by simplifying a few strict-syntax constructs there and switching `TitleScene`/`DeathScene` from hard preloads to runtime loading for the hero-select script.
- Continued Phase 1 persistence work by making `SaveManager` party-aware: full saves now store a `heroes` array plus `party_classes` and `local_hero_index`, load restores the whole party instead of collapsing back to one hero, and older single-hero saves still fall back through the legacy `hero` payload path.
- Wired the real continue flow onto that persistence work: `TitleScene` now prefers `SaveManager.load_full_game()` and routes successful continues through `LoadingScene` instead of jumping straight into `GameScene`, and `LoadingScene` now explicitly picks up restored `party_classes` when resuming a run.
- Added the first explicit co-op stair rule in `GameScene`: multi-hero runs now require all living party members to be standing on the same entrance/exit stairs before ascend/descend can fire, and floor-change artifact hooks now run for the whole party instead of only the acting hero.
- Continued co-op rules in moment-to-moment play: tile interactions are now hero-specific in `GameScene`, so any moving party member can trigger pickups and plant activation, gold pickup now charges the correct collector's equipped artifact, and failed inventory pickups are dropped back onto the floor instead of disappearing.
- Added the first internet-session infrastructure layer for multiplayer: new `NetworkManager` autoload owns ENet host/join/disconnect state, and `TitleScene` now exposes `Host Co-op` / `Join Co-op` entry points with a simple network panel for address, port, and player-cap setup plus live session-status feedback.
- Continued that internet layer into a real synchronized pre-game flow: `NetworkManager` now maintains a replicated lobby player list with ready state, `TitleScene` captures a local player name and transitions successful host/join sessions into a new `LobbyScene`, and `LobbyScene` displays connected players plus ready/start/leave controls as the first real shared online session screen.
- Extended the online pre-game flow into synchronized run setup: lobby start now moves every connected peer into `HeroSelectScene`, clients become waiting spectators there, and when the host presses Start the selected `party_classes` are broadcast through `NetworkManager` so all peers transition into `LoadingScene` with the same run-start configuration.
- Pushed online slot ownership into run bootstrap: `NetworkManager` now includes synchronized `player_infos` in the host's run-start config, and `LoadingScene` uses that to assign party hero names and set each client's initial focused hero index based on its connected peer ID.
- Added the first in-run online command seam: `Hero` now carries `owner_peer_id` and `hero_slot_index`, `LoadingScene` assigns those during online bootstrap, `NetworkManager` can now emit `online_action_requested(peer_id, slot_index, action)` to the host, and `GameScene` now applies host-side actions through a shared per-hero action path instead of only through local direct input.

- Tags: input, ui, qol
- Added explicit keyboard attack binds in `GameScene`: `A` and `F` now attack the first adjacent hostile target, with a warning if no adjacent enemy is available.
- Enlarged the popup map window again by letting `WndMap` use a much larger viewport fraction and reducing frame padding, so the opened map takes up more of the screen instead of remaining close to minimap size.
- Continued the fallback-item cleanup by fixing the Imp reward pool to use canonical `ring_of_sharpshooting` instead of the bad `ring_sharpshoot` identifier, and added generator aliases so older save data or stray legacy callsites still resolve to the real ring instead of a generic fallback item.
- Continued that cleanup in the Wandmaker quest by switching the rotberry request/item path from legacy `rotberry_seed` to canonical `seed_of_rotberry`, so the quest now uses the real seed ID end-to-end instead of depending on alias fallback behavior.
- Audited grass/level-generation feel and adjusted three high-signal mismatches: trampled grass now drops a real visible `Dewdrop` heap instead of instant auto-healing, standard rooms no longer spray large amounts of grass into every normal floor, and `Feeling.GRASS` now paints a more controlled mix of grass/high grass instead of turning whole standard-room interiors into blanket grass tiles.
- Continued the grass-system cleanup by replacing generic placeholder seed items with a real `SeedItem` class: generated seeds now support actual targeted planting, the item window shows a `Plant` action for seeds, and throwing seeds through the normal hero action path now plants/activates them correctly instead of treating them like inert generic items.
- Tightened the seed/plant flow further by making Wandmaker's rotberry quest item a real `SeedItem` instead of a generic item, and by removing the duplicate post-activation plant erase in `GameScene` so plant cleanup is owned consistently by `Plant.activate()`.
- Fixed a real Sad Ghost/Imp quest-tracking bug by extending `mob_defeated` to carry both `mob_name` and stable `mob_id`, then switching quest kill matching to use `mob_id` where appropriate. This fixes cases like `Gnoll Trickster` where the display name did not match the stored quest target identifier.
- Added a real plant render path: `GameScene` now tracks planted flora separately from terrain and items, and new `PlantSprite` nodes give planted seeds/plants a distinct on-map presentation instead of reading only as a `HIGH_GRASS` terrain change.
- Added plant placement/activation feedback on the effect layer: planting now shows a small burst and `Planted` status, and triggering major plants now emits plant-specific color/status VFX like `Regen`, `Armor`, `Burn!`, `Frozen`, `Poison`, `Rooted`, `Sleep`, `Warp`, `XP`, and `Haste`.
- Tightened three thinner plant mechanics: `Stormvine` now applies both `Rooted` and `Vertigo`, `Fadeleaf` now avoids obviously bad teleport destinations and emits `hero_moved` for hero teleports, and `Dreamfoil` now explicitly puts mobs back into the `SLEEPING` AI state instead of using a raw integer state write.

## 2026-05-08

- Tags: tooling, workflow, memory
- Added a lightweight repo-local memory system under `docs/memory/`.
- Added canonical files for active context, architecture map, decisions, lessons, backlog, and session-level change summaries.
- Added helper scripts to search memory quickly and append timestamped notes.
- Updated `CLAUDE.md` to point future work toward the concise memory layer before using large historical logs.
- Explicitly positioned the existing Claude-agent logs as legacy deep-reference sources beneath the new concise memory layer.
- Moved bulky historical logs from the repo root into `docs/history/` and added archive `README.md`, `INDEX.md`, and `SUMMARY.md` to preserve accessibility while reducing clutter.
- Added compact memory notes derived from the archive: `archive-backlog.md`, `system-summaries.md`, and `session-checklist.md`.
- Added focused topic notes for persistence, framework-readiness, and SPD-fidelity work.
- Started closing the original-SPD identification gap with a persistent `ItemCatalog` autoload, Journal catalog wiring, and automatic potion/scroll self-identification on use.
- Added a per-run `ItemAppearance` autoload so potions, scrolls, and rings now get deterministic unidentified appearances that persist through save/load.
- Added a persistent `DiscoveryCatalog` for enemies, traps, and plants, and expanded the Journal catalog beyond items-only.
- Extended `DiscoveryCatalog` and the Journal to track weapon enchantments and armor glyphs via proc events and identified inscribed gear.
- Discovery tracking now also records revealed enemies and interacted NPCs, so the Journal catalog is not limited to kills.
- Added groundwork for talents: `TalentData` definitions, hero-owned talent points and levels, save/load support, and read-only talent display in Hero Info.
- Added a spendable talent UI with `WndTalents`, wired it into Hero Info, and added reusable window-content refresh support so talent changes update live.
- Implemented the first live talent effects: Warrior `Hearty Meal`, Mage `Empowering Meal`, Rogue `Cached Rations`, Huntress `Nature's Bounty`, and Duelist `Adventurer's Intuition`.
- Implemented the class intuition pickup hooks: Warrior `Tested Hypothesis`, Mage `Scholar's Intuition`, Rogue `Thief's Intuition`, and Huntress `Survivalist's Intuition`.
- Fixed gold item generation so random gold piles now keep their SPD `items.png` sprite index, and updated the item detail window to use the real item icon instead of a color placeholder.
- Replaced several remaining color/text-only item previews with sheet-backed icons in the shop window, alchemy window, and generic item-selection window.
- Extended that UI art cleanup to picker-style rows as well, including the shop sell picker.
- Fixed the hero combat pipeline so invisibility-based surprise attacks resolve before breaking stealth, and hero combat now routes through equipped weapon enchantment and armor glyph procs.
- Implemented the first combat talent hooks: Rogue `Sucker Punch`, Duelist `Patient Strike`, and Mage `Backup Barrier`.
- Implemented targeted thrown-item resolution through the hero action system, added a real `Shoot` action for `Spirit Bow`, and made Huntress `Followup Strike` a live ranged-to-melee combat hook.
- Hooked Huntress click-to-attack flow into `Spirit Bow`, so clicking a visible distant enemy now auto-shoots instead of always auto-walking into melee when a clear bow shot is available.
- Hardened run-reset and scene-loading loops against stale freed hero/mob references by avoiding typed `Node` iteration over arrays that can still contain invalid instances after death/restart transitions.
- Formalized the Huntress `Spirit Bow` as a dedicated equipment slot instead of a backpack special-case, and updated inventory/status/hero-info UI to reflect the separate melee and bow loadout.
- Fixed a regression from the bow-slot formalization where ranged attacks animated but exited before resolving because `throw_item` still only accepted melee-weapon or backpack ownership, not the dedicated `spirit_bow` slot.
- Routed wand zaps through the hero action system so targeted wand use now consumes a proper turn instead of resolving instantly from the item window callback.
- Added basic use-based wand identification, with zap usage progress persisted through save/load.
- Made wands properly equippable in the `misc` slot and upgraded quickslot behavior so quickslotted wands enter targeting directly for faster SPD-style tool use.
- Rebuilt the alchemy recipe layer so seed-to-potion and stone-to-scroll recipes now use valid item IDs, and updated the alchemy window to operate on real `Recipe` results instead of broken placeholder dictionaries.
- Wired `Alchemize` to actually open `WndAlchemy` anywhere through `EventBus.show_window`, and hooked the Alchemist's Toolkit into crafting so it gains progress from completed recipes.
- Implemented the missing hero `search` action by routing toolbar search through the normal hero action pipeline and calling the existing secret-door/hidden-trap reveal logic, so searching now spends a real turn and can actually discover adjacent secrets without duplicate signal handling.
- Improved search and door parity further: bumping/clicking adjacent impassable terrain now searches instead of failing silently, adjacent doors use the shared `Door` interaction logic, and keyboard `S` now triggers search directly.
- Added explicit search feedback in the main action animation path, so using search now shows a visible status/particle effect on the hero even when no secret is found.
- Restored base dewdrop generation from trampling high grass, instead of limiting dewdrops to the Huntress talent path; `Nature's Bounty` now boosts the baseline dew chance and can still add seed finds.
- Rebuilt the Sad Ghost quest flow: first interaction now starts the quest and spawns the target mob, kill tracking completes the objective, rewards use the standard quest reward window with real generated items, and the ghost now departs cleanly through the shared quest infrastructure.
- Fixed mimic visuals in both states: revealed mimics now use the original SPD `mimic.png` mob art, and disguised mimics now render using their configured fake loot item instead of a hardcoded generic potion icon.
- Removed the artificial per-visible-mob post-action delay from the turn manager so enemies still resolve in initiative order and update visually, but no longer add a noticeable pause before control returns to the player.
- Upgraded resting from a hidden one-turn wait into a real HUD action: the toolbar now has a Rest button, resting starts continuous auto-waiting, refuses to start while enemies are visible, and interrupts correctly on damage or when enemies come into view.
- Fixed secret-room entrance generation for tunneled branch connections: secret rooms attached as `neighbors` now get real `SECRET_DOOR` endpoint tiles instead of plain carved openings, so searching can reveal their entrances properly.
- Fixed the quickslot HUD/inventory flow: quickslot capacity now matches the toolbar, assignments persist in belongings serialization, the HUD refreshes from real quickslot state, and item windows now open a slot-selection sub-window instead of always assigning to slot 1.
- Replaced text-only quickslot labels in the toolbar with real `items.png` icon rendering plus slot-number overlays, fixing clipped item names and bringing quickslot visuals in line with the rest of the item UI.
- Fixed popup centering and basic viewport responsiveness: `WndBase` windows now fit within the current viewport and center themselves properly, the HUD now switches to a more compact portrait-friendly layout, and the hero-select scene no longer forces size on a fully anchored root control.
- Fixed dynamically spawned mob visibility for cases like Swarm splits: `GameScene` now ensures any alive mob present in the level but missing a sprite gets a `MobSprite` during turn refresh, preventing invisible logical enemies after split/spawn events.
- Expanded SPD sprite-sheet coverage in `MobSprite` for many valid mob and NPC IDs that already had art assets on disk, reducing cases where alerted or newly seen entities fell back to procedural placeholder art with health bars.
- Improved death presentation: generic character death now fades and settles instead of shrinking away aggressively, hero death now has a distinct collapse animation, and the death-scene transition waits long enough for that animation to read on screen.
- Closed several remaining item/content compatibility gaps at the generator boundary by normalizing old SPD-style IDs (for example `scroll_of_identify` and `potion_of_healing`) and adding proper quest-item generation for the wandmaker request items instead of falling through to generic unknown-item placeholders.
- Improved alert AI behavior: alarm/noisemaker-style alerts now propagate a disturbance position through `Level.alert_all_mobs()`, mobs enter hunting with a meaningful `target_pos`, and alerted mobs show the alert emote instead of merely flipping state silently.
- Tightened asset fidelity around mimic disguises by switching their fake loot IDs to the actual generator-supported IDs, which keeps disguise visuals aligned with the real item art path instead of relying on alias/fallback behavior.
- Continued the same cleanup pass by converting a few remaining live legacy scroll IDs at the callsite level (hero starter loadout and Unstable Spellbook effect table), so those paths no longer depend on alias translation.
- Improved mob behavior fidelity further: alerted mobs with no visible hero now investigate the disturbance location instead of immediately dropping back to wandering when `target` is null.
- Added SPD sprite-sheet coverage for Yog's fist variants so those boss entities no longer need procedural fallback art.
- Fixed animated/golden statue weapon generation by routing them through a real tiered melee-weapon helper in `Generator` instead of the invalid `melee_weapon` ID, so statue combat and reward drops now use actual enchantable weapons rather than generic fallback items.
- Fixed the shop inventory path so the shopkeeper now stocks real generated items with canonical IDs instead of placeholder `Item` objects with bad IDs like `scroll_mirror_image` and `potion_healing`, improving both purchased-item behavior and item-art fidelity in shop UI.
- Implemented the missing sheep-summon path shared by `Stone of Flock` and `Wooly Bomb`: added a real temporary `Sheep` mob blocker and wired both consumables to spawn sheep on valid open cells instead of only printing placeholder messages.
- Replaced three remaining spell placeholders with live behavior: `Recycle` now opens item selection and transmutes consumables into others of the same broad class, `Curse Infusion` now curses and upgrades a selected item, and `Summon Elemental` now creates a temporary allied fire or frost elemental that follows the hero and attacks enemies.
- Cleaned a large launch-warning batch across gameplay, UI, and utility scripts: fixed unused parameters/locals, several local-name shadowing issues, and the integer-division warnings reported from `char.gd`, `scroll.gd`, `bomb.gd`, `wand.gd`, `item_slot.gd`, `ballistica.gd`, and related files.
- Implemented two more previously placeholder runestones: `Stone of Enchantment` now applies a random weapon enchantment to the equipped melee weapon or spirit bow, and `Stone of Augmentation` now lets the player pick a weapon/armor target and choose a real augment through a new augmentation selector window.
- Fixed missing enemy attack animations by making mobs record explicit last visible action metadata (`move` vs `attack`) and having `GameScene` animate from that data instead of trying to infer attacks from the mob's post-action `target` state.
- Extended that mob-attack animation fix to cover misses too, so visible enemy melee swings now animate whether they hit or miss instead of only when `on_attack_hit()` fired.
- Replaced the immediate-detonation bomb placeholder with real fuse timing: armed bombs now live on the `Level`, tick once per hero round via `TurnManager.round_completed`, detonate before enemy turns, and persist through `RegularLevel` save/load.
- Added visible telegraphing for armed bombs: `GameScene` now renders a separate floor sprite for any bomb in `Level.pending_bombs`, applies normal fog/visited visibility rules, and shows a small `Fuse` popup when a bomb becomes active.
- Implemented the real cast path for `Unstable Spellbook`: it now instantiates the chosen scroll ID and applies that scroll's `read_scroll()` effect directly to the hero instead of only logging the chosen effect and emitting a generic event.
- Wired equipped artifact hooks into the real hero loop: `Hero.execute_action()` now calls `artifact.on_turn(self)` each hero turn, and search actions now call `artifact.on_search()` when supported, which makes artifacts like `Talisman of Foresight` actually charge and apply their passive/turn-based behaviors in play.
- Implemented a real terrain-growth effect for `Sandals of Nature`: passive charged movement can now convert `EMPTY`/`EMBERS` into `GRASS` and upgrade grass into `HIGH_GRASS`, while activation now triggers a small burst of nearby growth instead of only printing flavor text.
- Completed the basic `Sandals of Nature` charge loop in the UI: seed items now show a `Feed` action in `WndItem` when the sandals are equipped, which consumes one seed from the stack and routes it into `SandalsOfNature.feed_seed(...)`.
- Implemented the real `Master Thieves' Armband` loop: positive gold gain now routes through `on_gold_pickup()` when the armband is equipped, and activation now performs an actual adjacent-shopkeeper theft attempt that steals a real stock item on success or forces the shopkeeper to flee on failure.
- Fixed a boss-AI timing mismatch: custom `_act_hunting()` overrides for `Goo`, `Tengu`, `DM-300`, `King of Dwarves`, and `Yog-Dzewa` now explicitly spend turn/move/attack time for their special actions, restoring fair cooldown behavior under the shared turn system.
- Replaced the placeholder thief/bandit steal behavior with real inventory theft: `Crazy Thief` and `Crazy Bandit` now remove an actual backpack item (or one unit from a stack), flee after stealing it, and drop that stolen item on death instead of only toggling a boolean placeholder.
- Implemented a real first-pass `Necromancer` AI loop: it now acquires a hero target, raises one linked `Skeleton`, keeps that skeleton focused on the hero, periodically heals or hastes it, retreats when pressured, and destroys the linked skeleton when the necromancer dies.
- Fixed the same turn-cost mismatch across several non-boss ranged mobs: `Gnoll Shaman`, `Scorpio`, `Gnoll Trickster`, and `DM-100` now explicitly spend attack/move/idle time inside their custom `_act_hunting()` logic instead of performing ranged/kiting behavior without consuming proper turn cost.
- Tightened two more special-mob behaviors: `Succubus` teleport-escape now consumes the turn instead of teleporting and then also taking a full normal action, and `Stone Golem` now emits `hero_moved` when its on-hit teleport displaces the hero so the rest of the game state/UI stays in sync.
- Improved cave/prison special behavior: `Spinner` now tracks the hero's last seen position and shoots predictive webs that also seed a real `WebBlob`, instead of only dropping `WEB` terrain on the hero's current tile, and `Prison Guard` chain-pull now consumes proper attack time.
- Implemented the missing `Timekeeper's Hourglass` scheduler effect in `TurnManager`: while the hourglass freeze is active, non-hero actors are skipped by the turn loop and still advanced through cooldown accounting, so time-freeze now actually stops enemy actions instead of only tracking local artifact state.
- Wired artifact `on_floor_change()` hooks into stair transitions in `GameScene`, so per-floor artifacts like `Dried Rose` can now reset/refresh their state when moving between dungeon levels instead of carrying stale per-floor state forever.
- Hardened the save/backtrack reconstruction path: `LevelFactory` now supports subclass instantiation without generation, `SaveManager` recreates the correct level subclass on cold load, `Level` now serializes/deserializes mobs/traps/plants/blobs/armed bombs directly, base `Mob` deserialization now restores core combat/AI state instead of being a stub, and cached-level loading in `LoadingScene` no longer stacks fresh quest/shop spawns on top of restored level state.
- Fixed Sad Ghost/NPC interaction regressions: adjacent NPC clicks in `GameScene` now route to `interact` instead of the generic adjacent-attack path, and `SadGhost` now uses its own wandering turn loop/state handling instead of inheriting the passive NPC act path that left it stationary and non-behaving.
- Fixed a death-phase turn-loop bug: `TurnManager` now aborts the async enemy-processing loop if the hero dies or the `GameScene` has already ended, and `GameScene._on_hero_died()` now explicitly drops turn-manager processing flags so the floor cannot keep resolving visible enemy turns during the death-scene delay.
- Continued save hardening: `SaveManager` no longer restores blobs twice after the level serializer absorbed them, hero/mob active buff state now persists through save/load using script-path-based buff reconstruction, and `Necromancer` now relinks its summoned skeleton after level load using saved `actor_id` references instead of dead runtime instance IDs.
- Closed a follow-on persistence hazard in actor identity: deserializing an `Actor` now bumps the global `_next_id` floor to at least `actor_id + 1`, preventing newly spawned mobs/allies/projected actors after load from colliding with restored actor IDs.
- Extended persistence into the quest/NPC layer: base `NPC` save/load now restores quest flags and re-registers restored quest NPCs with `QuestHandler`, and custom state for `Sad Ghost`, `Wandmaker`, `Blacksmith`, and `Shopkeeper` now persists instead of falling back to generic mob-only restoration.
- Repaired the Imp quest path: `Ambitious Imp` now has a coherent single-ring reward flow with real generation, reward handoff, departure, and save/load state, instead of referencing undefined reward fields/functions; also fixed `QuestHandler` to call `Wandmaker.create_quest_item(...)` instead of the non-existent `create_quest_seed(...)` when spawning the wandmaker quest item.
- Continued the quest/NPC runtime cleanup: `Wandmaker` now generates real wand rewards with canonical IDs, uses correct inventory-ID/quantity checks for handing over quest items, and only completes the quest when a reward is actually chosen; `WndQuestReward` now emits an explicit reward-selection signal so quest NPCs no longer incorrectly depart just because the window closed. `Blacksmith` now uses a real `WndReforge` flow, consumes ore through quantity-safe inventory removal, and applies the actual reforge rule/cleanup through the NPC logic instead of leaving reforging as a dead text-only branch.
- Added a real root `README.md` for the project, documenting what the port is, current `0.1.1` status, how to run it in Godot 4.5, the repository layout, current goals, and the main known rough edges.
- Closed a major quest-facing UI/runtime gap in the shop flow: `Shopkeeper.interact()` now opens the existing `WndShop` instead of dumping stock to the message log, `WndShop` now delegates purchases and sales back through the `Shopkeeper` so inventory/persistence state stays authoritative, and shop sell pricing now uses actual item value instead of a missing `price` property on inventory items.
- Implemented a real first-pass `Dried Rose`: added a summonable allied `RoseGhost` mob, wired the artifact to spawn and track that ghost instead of only toggling local flags, added cleanup on floor change, added post-load relinking from the equipped artifact back to the restored summoned ghost, and added SPD ghost-sheet art mapping for the ally.
- Implemented a real targeted first pass for `Ethereal Chains`: activation now enters the existing targeting mode, then either pulls the hero to a valid open destination with a clear chain path or yanks a visible enemy toward the hero into the nearest valid adjacent landing cell, spending charge only on successful use instead of only emitting placeholder text.
- Continued save/load hardening for special mobs: `Swarm` now persists whether it has already split, and `Mimic` now persists disguise state, fake item appearance, and stored loot contents instead of restoring as a generic/default mimic after load.
- Continued prison-mob persistence hardening: `Crazy Thief` and `Crazy Bandit` now serialize their stolen item payloads so post-load fleeing, death drops, and item recovery still work instead of losing the stolen inventory state across save/load.
- Continued summon/special-mob persistence hardening: temporary `Bee`, `Summoned Elemental`, `Sheep`, and `Animated Statue` state now survives save/load, including hero-link relinking for allied summons and weapon restoration for statues.
- Continued cooldown/phase persistence hardening for stateful mobs and bosses: `Warlock`, `DM-100`, `Spinner`, `Evil Eye`, `DM-300`, `King of Dwarves`, `Tengu`, and `Yog-Dzewa` now restore their active cooldown and phase fields instead of resuming from default encounter state after load.
- Extended that persistence hardening across the remaining encounter mobs with custom runtime state: `Shaman`, `Gnoll Trickster`, `Scorpio`, `DM-200`, `DM-201`, `Ripper Demon`, `Dwarf Monk`, and `Yog Fist` now restore their cooldown/type/combo/leap state correctly after save/load.
- Continued save/load hardening into buff state: custom counters and embedded state now persist for `Chill`, `Bleeding`, `Ooze`, `Burning`, `Regeneration`, `Combo`, and several subclass buffs including `Berserker Rage`, `Champion Dual Wield`, `Freerunner Momentum`, `Gladiator Combo`, `Monk Flurry`, `Sniper Mark`, `Warden Barkskin`, and `Assassin Preparation`.
- Fixed a real quest restore sequencing bug in `SaveManager`: saved `QuestHandler` state is now deserialized before level/NPC reconstruction, so restored quest NPCs can re-register themselves without that registration being wiped by a later quest-state reset during load.
- Fixed a live NPC interaction regression: hero `interact` actions now actually call adjacent NPC `interact(...)` handlers instead of only opening doors, and keyboard bump movement now routes into NPC interaction instead of trying to attack passive quest/shop characters.
- Added a clear message when a floor has the `DARK` feeling, so intentionally darker floors are surfaced as gameplay state rather than looking like a silent visual regression.
- Wired missing gameplay/UI keybinds in `GameScene` and `HUD`: `I` now toggles inventory, `M` toggles the minimap, `R` triggers resting, `Esc` opens settings when no popup is active, and number-row `1`-`6` now activate quickslots.
- Replaced the old map toggle behavior with a real centered map overlay: `M` and the map toolbar button now open `WndMap`, which shows an enlarged current-floor map instead of merely hiding the small corner minimap widget.
- Hardened `Minimap` for the new popup-map path by making its backing image/texture initialize lazily, fixing the null-image crash when the enlarged map window populates a fresh minimap before `_ready()` runs.
- Restored several spell behaviors that had regressed to placeholders: `Recycle` now transmutes a selected consumable into another of the same broad class, `Curse Infusion` now curses and upgrades a selected weapon/armor while enabling the infusion bonus, and `Summon Elemental` now spawns a real allied fire or frost elemental instead of only printing a message.
- Added a real `Level.spawn_mob(...)` helper and used it to close several trap-effect gaps: `Flock Trap` and `Guardian Trap` now have a working spawn path, and `Summoning Trap` now actually spawns hostile mobs instead of stopping at a placeholder comment.
- Implemented the missing blob visual layer in `GameScene`: active gas/fire/web cells from `Level.blobs` now render as visibility-aware colored overlays between the terrain and entity layers, so toxic gas, paralytic gas, confusion gas, fire, webs, and similar blob effects are finally visible on the map instead of existing only in gameplay logic.
- Tuned that new blob overlay so it only renders on currently visible cells instead of faintly tinting previously visited ones, which avoids making normal floors read darker than intended; also updated `CharSprite.play_attack()`/`play_operate()` so heroes and mobs visibly face the target of an attack or interaction even when they do not move first.
- Closed the next parity gaps: `Reclaim Trap` now creates a real serialized `TrappedItem` that can be placed later instead of only removing floor terrain, Halls floors now cap the hero's sight radius using the original depth-based rule, lingering bad loot IDs in `Necromancer` and `Scorpio` were normalized to canonical item IDs, and `Ballistica`/wand targeting now support ignoring soft solids like doors/webs when requested instead of treating every solid the same.
- Fixed a concrete duplicate-food regression: `Rat` loot was still using the invalid legacy ID `food_ration`, which produced a generic fallback item with the wrong name/art; it now uses canonical `ration`, so food drops resolve to the real SPD-backed ration item consistently.
- Enlarged the popup map window by making `WndMap` scale from the current viewport instead of using a fixed small multiplier, so the opened map now takes much more of the screen on desktop while still fitting smaller/mobile-like windows.
- Continued the legacy-item-ID cleanup pass: `Swarm` now drops canonical `healing` instead of invalid `healing_potion`, and `Skeleton` no longer uses the fake `weapon_t2` ID at all — it now rolls a real tier-2 weapon drop directly, preventing more generic fallback items and wrong art/name cases.
- Tuned early-floor presentation and keyboard flow: Sewer floors no longer roll the `DARK` feeling on depths 1-2, which avoids “first/second floor looks broken” reports, and `Enter` / numpad `Enter` now use stairs contextually so keyboard play can descend or ascend without requiring symbol keys.
- Continued the item-fallback cleanup by making `metal_shard` a real misc item in the generator instead of an unknown ID, so `DM-100`/`DM-201` shard drops no longer resolve to generic placeholder items.
- Audited and corrected a major water-generation parity gap: standard rooms no longer flood their entire interior on `Feeling.WATER`, and `StandardPainter` now paints region-style smoothed water patches for Sewers, Caves, and City using fill/smoothness values aligned with upstream SPD instead of the previous flat random scatter.
- Audited mob spawn IDs and fixed a concrete fallback-enemy path: `Sad Ghost` quest targets `fetid_rat` and `great_crab` now resolve to real mob classes in `MobFactory` instead of falling back to the base `Mob`, and a new `FetidRat` sewer mob class was added so the ghost quest can no longer produce a generic “mob” enemy with procedural fallback art/name.
- Hardened `MobFactory` so unknown explicit `mob_id` requests no longer silently instantiate the generic base `Mob`; they now return `null` with a warning, while weighted random regional spawns still fall back safely to `Rat` only if a selected table entry somehow resolves to nothing.
- Fixed the remaining Imp presentation gap: `ambitious_imp` now uses the original SPD `demon.png` sprite sheet in `MobSprite` instead of falling back to generated art.
- Improved plant presentation without inventing new assets: `PlantSprite` now reuses the original SPD seed icons from `items.png` as embedded plant blossoms where available, instead of drawing every plant entirely procedurally. This repo does not include dedicated upstream plant sprites, so the seed icons are now the closest canonical art path.
- Upgraded blob/gas/fire/web presentation in `GameScene`: the blob overlay layer no longer draws flat tinted rectangles per cell and now renders layered puff/strand shapes for gas, fire, and webs so room-filling effects read more like visible hazards and less like placeholder map tinting.
- Replaced the `WndHeroInfo` portrait placeholder with a real SPD-backed hero portrait: the info window now crops the hero's actual class sprite sheet instead of showing a flat class-color block, bringing that UI path onto the same original-art route as the status pane and hero selection screen.
- Closed a remaining summon-art gap: `Mirror Image` now records the copied hero class and `GameScene` renders it with a tinted `HeroSprite` instead of a generic `MobSprite`, so the summon now looks like a spectral copy of the player rather than falling back to generated enemy art.
- Tightened hero portrait fidelity further by switching both `StatusPane` and `WndHeroInfo` to use the real SPD `avatars.png` portrait sheet instead of cropped in-world sprite frames, so hero-facing UI now uses the original portrait art path rather than a gameplay-sprite approximation.
- Fixed a live buff regression: `Burning` no longer calls `take_damage()` with an invalid third argument, which was crashing when fire-based potion effects or other burning damage ticked on the hero.
- Fixed secret-trap clarity on activation: `Trap.activate()` now force-reveals hidden traps before they fire, and `GameScene` now refreshes the terrain/fog state when a trap triggers instead of only playing the trap sound, so poison/worn dart traps should become visibly revealed once they go off.
- Bumped the actual game-facing version to `0.1.1`: updated the title-screen version label and About text, added `config/version="0.1.1"` to `project.godot`, and set export `file_version` / `product_version` metadata so UI, project settings, and exported builds no longer disagree.
- Added a durable co-op implementation roadmap for `0.1.2` in `docs/memory/multiplayer-roadmap.md`, defining the project target as host-authoritative online co-op for up to 4 players, with phased implementation starting from single-process multi-hero support before any networking layer is added.
- Started Phase 1 co-op refactoring in code: `GameManager` now has a real party helper API (`get_primary_hero`, `get_local_hero`, `get_active_heroes`, `add_hero`, `remove_hero`, `set_local_hero_index`, `is_party_run`) plus `local_hero_index`, giving multiplayer work a first-class seam instead of only raw `hero` / `heroes` fields.
- Moved the first low-risk consumers onto that seam: `TurnManager`, `StatusPane`, and parts of `HUD` now read from party-aware hero accessors instead of hardcoding `GameManager.hero` everywhere, which begins separating “local focused hero” from “primary hero” behavior.
- Bumped the project branch target to `0.1.2`: updated title/About UI strings, `project.godot` version metadata, export file/product version metadata, and top-level `README.md` milestone text so the repo and game no longer still advertise `0.1.1`.
- Continued Phase 1 in `GameScene`: added a local-hero accessor and moved the main input, action submission, camera/FOV refresh, auto-walk, item pickup, plant activation, trap-refresh, stairs, targeting, and hero-damage/death presentation paths onto that seam instead of hardcoding `GameManager.hero` throughout the interaction loop.
- Hardened the `GameScene` launch path after the co-op refactor introduced parser brittleness: removed most custom-class type annotations from `game_scene.gd`, replaced direct `NPC`/`CharSprite` type checks with duck-typed behavior checks, and updated `MainScene`, `TitleScene`, and `HeroSelectScene` to load `game_scene.gd` dynamically instead of requiring the global `GameScene` class at parse time just to open menus or continue a run.
- Continued that parser-hardening pass into `GameScene` and `HUD`: `game_scene.gd` now instantiates its major helpers and sprites by script path instead of direct custom-class constructors, no longer depends on `Gold`/`Wand`/`MissileWeapon`/`Bomb` type checks for core flows, and `hud.gd` now dynamically instantiates its subcomponents and popup windows while using duck-typed quickslot/window handling instead of direct `Wnd*`, `Wand`, and `Belongings` class references.
- Continued `v0.1.2` co-op groundwork in the actual turn model: `TurnManager` now tracks the current input hero explicitly, `GameManager.get_local_hero()` follows that active hero automatically, and hero-round accounting now treats a round as “all currently registered heroes have acted once” instead of implicitly assuming a single hero. `Hero` now reports itself back through `hero_action_complete(self)`, and key visibility/UI readers like `FogOfWar`, `WndMap`, `WndInventory`, `WndHeroInfo`, `WndAlchemy`, `WndShop`, `WndTalents`, and `WndQuestReward` now read the focused/local hero through the new party-aware accessor instead of hardcoding `GameManager.hero`.
- Added an observable local-hero focus layer for the multiplayer groundwork: `GameManager` now exposes `local_hero_changed`, `get_hero_index(...)`, and `cycle_local_hero_focus(...)`; `TurnManager` updates `GameManager` when a different hero reaches the input phase; and both `HUD` and `GameScene` now react to focus changes so the status UI, FOV, fog, and camera follow the currently active local/input hero cleanly.
- Added hero-specific event payload groundwork for co-op: `EventBus` now exposes `hero_moved_detailed`, `hero_damaged_detailed`, and `hero_died_detailed`; the current hero movement/damage/death emitters now send detailed payloads for the actual hero involved while the legacy single-hero signals only mirror the currently focused/local hero; and `GameScene` plus `Minimap` now prefer the detailed hero signals so future multi-hero runs can distinguish local/focused hero updates from other party members cleanly.
- Added the first explicit local-party focus control: `GameScene` now uses `Tab` to cycle the locally focused hero when a party run is active, and `StatusPane` now shows a small `Focus: P#/N` indicator for the currently focused party member. The portrait path also now reads the focused hero's actual `hero_class` instead of falling back to the run-global class field.
- Added the first lightweight party HUD strip in `HUD`: party runs now show compact top-row hero buttons with class/HP summary, the currently focused hero is highlighted, and clicking a party entry changes the local hero focus through `GameManager.set_local_hero_index(...)`. The row also repositions responsively for narrower/mobile-like layouts.
- Centralized party bootstrap for future co-op runs: `GameManager` now owns `party_classes`, `set_party_classes(...)`, `get_party_classes()`, `create_party_heroes()`, and `replace_party(...)`, while `LoadingScene` now creates party heroes through that API and places the whole party around the staircase anchor instead of only a single hero. `HeroSelectScene` and `MainScene` now route new-game startup back through `LoadingScene`, so the run bootstrap path is unified instead of duplicating one-hero setup logic across scenes.
- Continued the multiplayer roadmap’s co-op readability pass by adding in-world ally identification: `CharSprite` now supports a persistent ally label separate from temporary emotes, and `GameScene` now applies party-aware labels like `P1 Alice`/`P2 Mage` to hero sprites in multi-hero runs, with color highlighting for the focused hero, the locally owned hero, and fallen heroes so party members are easier to distinguish directly on the dungeon floor.
- Continued that readability pass into the HUD: the online-state banner now uses the same `P# Name` hero identity format as the new floor labels, including `Your turn: ...`, `Waiting for ...`, and `Spectating ...` states, and the party row now marks focus, active turn, and local ownership consistently so control/ownership is readable without guessing from context.
- Continued the readability pass into the world presentation itself: `CharSprite` now supports subtle ground rings, and `GameScene` now applies them to party heroes in multi-hero runs so active turn, local ownership, focus, ally state, and fallen heroes are distinguishable at a glance even before reading labels or HUD text.
- Continued online authority polish by adding explicit host-to-client action rejection feedback: `NetworkManager` now has a dedicated `online_action_rejected` channel, host-side slot/turn validation now sends concrete rejection reasons like `You do not control that hero.` or `It is not that hero's turn.`, and `GameScene` now surfaces those failures to clients through the message log plus a `Blocked` status popup instead of silently dropping the input.
- Continued authority hardening in the client UI: `Toolbar` now supports action-only disabling, and `HUD` now dims/disables inventory, wait, rest, search, and quickslot controls whenever the local player does not currently own the active hero in an online run, while still leaving passive controls like map/settings usable. The guarded HUD entry points also now show clear warnings instead of opening action-heavy UI out of turn.
- Extended that authority clarity into the raw input loop: `GameScene` now still allows passive keys like `Tab`, `I`, `M`, and `Esc` while out of turn, but online clients now get explicit `Wait` / spectating / wrong-turn feedback for blocked action keys and map clicks instead of silent dead input when another player owns the active hero.
- Reduced online snapshot harshness on the client by changing same-floor host snapshots from full scene rebuilds to incremental entity sync in `GameScene`: heroes and mobs now keep their existing sprites and animate toward corrected positions when possible, while full teardown/rebuild is reserved for real floor/depth transitions.
- Improved remote combat presentation during online snapshot sync: `Hero` and `Mob` now serialize lightweight `last_visible_action` / `last_visible_target_pos` state, and `GameScene` uses snapshot deltas to replay fresh remote attack/interact animations plus damage/heal feedback on clients instead of only updating bars and final positions.
- Expanded that remote action replay beyond melee: heroes now record visible `throw_item` and `zap_wand` actions too, and `GameScene` now replays ranged/utility snapshot deltas with thrown-projectile arcs, wand lightning, search pings, and interact/operate animations on clients so other players’ turns read much more like real actions instead of silent state corrections.
- Tightened online movement-event presentation on non-host clients: hero_moved world events no longer only nudge a sprite, and now also drive immediate camera/FOV/fog/tile-visibility refresh when the moved hero is the currently focused hero. This uses a client-preview visibility refresh path that respects sprite preview positions instead of snapping mobs back to stale authoritative positions before the next host snapshot arrives.
- Added held-key movement repeat in GameScene: when an arrow/numpad/vi movement key is held, the acting hero now repeats movement on a fixed 0.5s cadence while input is available, instead of requiring repeated taps. The repeat path is processed for both offline play and online clients, but still respects targeting mode, auto-walk, and turn ownership.
- Refined held-key movement repeat in GameScene: repeat cadence is now 0.25s instead of 0.5s, and overlapping movement keys now fall back correctly. Holding one direction, pressing a second, then releasing the second will resume repeating the still-held original direction instead of stopping movement entirely.
- Fixed delayed hero equipment visuals: equipping or unequipping armor no longer waits for a depth transition to update the hero sprite. GameScene now reapplies armor-tier visuals to live hero sprites on equipment change events and during online snapshot hero-sync, so local and remote loadout changes refresh immediately.
- Continued multiplayer QoL in the online setup flow: NetworkManager now exposes ready-count summaries, LobbyScene now shows ready progress plus [You] ownership markers in the player list, and the host start button surfaces readiness state directly instead of looking generically disabled. HeroSelectScene now also shows clearer host/client setup messaging, including ready counts for the host and the actual host player name for waiting clients.
- Matched the co-op lobby to the title screen background treatment: LobbyScene now uses the same SPD parallax dungeon layers and the same absolute-time scroll model as TitleScene, so navigating between menu and lobby no longer resets to a different background phase. Also fixed the lobby layout by centering the main panel and changing the action buttons to a 3-column grid, which restores balanced left/right margins instead of letting the panel stretch visually to the right.
- Reworked HeroSelectScene to share the same title-style parallax dungeon background treatment as the main menu and co-op lobby, and switched the layout to a framed two-column presentation: a left info/selection column over a translucent panel and a right framed splash-art column for the selected hero. The shared background now uses absolute-time parallax scrolling so menu-to-lobby-to-hero-select navigation no longer visibly resets the backdrop phase.
- Unified the hero-select two-column layout into one framed composition: HeroSelectScene now draws a single outer panel behind both the left info column and the right splash-art column, so it reads like one box in the same style family as the co-op lobby instead of two separate floating panels. The left column was softened into an internal section, while the right splash panel remains inset within the shared frame.
- Tightened the hero-select presentation again: the framed UI now matches the co-op lobby width more closely, and the right splash-art pane was narrowed into a taller portrait-style window so the hero art is cropped toward the center instead of reading like a wide banner. Also added a real Settings button to the title screen that opens the same wnd_settings.gd popup used in-game, so music volume and other runtime settings are accessible directly from the main menu.
- Fixed the hero-select framed layout so the actual selection controls live inside the shared outer box instead of being absolute-positioned on the scene root. The left-side title, class buttons, descriptions, party controls, and action buttons are now parented under the left panel section, and the splash pane is positioned as an internal section of the same framed container.
- Fixed the hero-select frame sections to use plain `Panel` controls instead of `PanelContainer`, so the right-side splash no longer auto-expands over the whole frame and the left-side hero choice controls can render beside it as intended.
- Tightened the hero-select splash pane so it behaves like a true right-half portrait panel: widened it to occupy the right half of the shared frame, aligned it to the frame midpoint, removed the inner image margin, and enabled clipping so the splash art is center-cropped inside the pane instead of spilling past the frame.
- Centered the hero-select splash crop explicitly inside the right pane by manually sizing the image to covered-fit the panel and offsetting it to the pane center, so portraits with empty left/right margins now keep the hero centered in the cropped frame.
- Removed the local party-size controls from `HeroSelectScene`, so the standard new-game flow is now a single-hero class choice screen again while online party size remains driven by actual lobby population.
- Reworked `LobbyScene` into the same framed split layout as hero select: a left information/control column inside the shared box and a right splash-preview column. The right side now stacks 1-4 centered, clipped splash panels vertically based on the current lobby population, so two-player lobbies split the right half into top/bottom portraits and four-player lobbies split it into four equal portrait bands.
- Constrained the co-op lobby host/player action controls to the left column width by changing the lobby action grid to a 2-column layout with column-sized buttons, so the controls no longer overflow the framed left half.
- Added a real persistent `PlayerProfile` autoload, which now owns the player name used for multiplayer identity and profile summary data. TitleScene now includes a `Player Profile` section with name editing, achievement/ranking summary, and badge access, and first-time players are prompted to choose a name before using the rest of the menu flow.
- Rankings entries now store and display `player_name`, so the new profile identity carries through into persistent run history instead of only existing in multiplayer settings.
- Tightened the co-op lobby left column spacing further by shrinking the content width and button grid to leave a larger gutter from the center split.
- Removed the remaining party-loadout presentation from the normal single-player hero select flow. `HeroSelectScene` now hides party slot/loadout UI, summary, and network notice when not in online setup, so `New Game` reads as a true single-hero choice screen while the online flow can still surface coordinated class setup separately.
- Reworked the co-op lobby action layout so the important controls sit in predictable corners: `Leave` now anchors the bottom-left action slot, while the bottom-right slot is used for `Ready Up` on clients and `Start Run` for the host. The player list was also simplified from `P1 Name [Host] [You] - ...` down to cleaner `Name [Host] - ...` labels.
- Removed join-code language from the co-op join flow. The title-screen join panel now uses `Host IP` plus port directly, the lobby no longer exposes `Copy Join Code`, and host state messaging now surfaces the host IP instead of a generated join code.
- Restyled `RankingsScene` to match the newer menu pages more closely by giving it the same parallax dungeon background treatment and a centered framed panel for the list and actions instead of the older flat full-screen layout.
- Tuned `WndSettings` toward the same visual language with a roomier layout and warmer label treatment so the shared settings window reads more consistently alongside the newer menu/profile surfaces.
- Audited the main-menu submenu action language and standardized several remaining panels: the co-op lobby now uses `Back` instead of `Leave`, the join panel is reduced to a simple host-IP join flow with `Back` / `Join`, the profile panel now uses secondary links above a bottom `Back` / `Save` row, and the About panel now uses `Back` language instead of `Close`.
- Fixed the remaining main-menu consistency gaps: `HeroSelectScene` now keeps `Back` on the left and `Start` on the right in the standard new-game flow, and the title-screen settings entry no longer opens the animated in-game popup window. `TitleScene` now has its own framed `Settings` submenu panel with the same `Back` / `Save` bottom-row structure and the same static menu-page behavior as the other title submenus.
- Hardened standard floor generation against unreachable stairs. `RegularLevel` now validates that a real path exists from the entrance to `exit_pos` immediately after painting/flag-map build, and aborts generation if the stairs-down room ended up graph-disconnected. That forces a regenerate instead of allowing a softlocked floor like the sealed depth-4 case.
- Tightened the generation retry path in `LevelFactory`: floor creation now gets multiple full regeneration attempts before the emergency fallback room is used. That means a rejected unreachable layout now normally rerolls into another real floor instead of immediately collapsing to the plain single-room fallback.
- Continued multiplayer authority hardening through NPC interaction by making the shop flow owner-targeted and host-authoritative. `NetworkManager` now supports targeted online UI events, `Shopkeeper` sends remote shop-open events to the interacting hero's owner instead of opening host-local UI, `WndShop` routes online buy/sell actions through `request_hero_action`, and `GameScene` now resolves `shop_buy` / `shop_sell` on the host and pushes targeted shop refresh events back to the owning client.
- Extended that same owner-targeted UI / host-authoritative pattern to quest reward windows. `Ghost`, `Imp`, and `Wandmaker` now send targeted reward-open UI events for remote owners, `WndQuestReward` routes online selections through a new `claim_quest_reward` action instead of granting items locally, and `GameScene` now resolves the reward claim on the host and invokes the NPC completion callback there.
- Added an `Exit to Menu` button to the in-game settings window, so players can leave directly from settings without backing out through the pause menu first.
- Continued the NPC interaction authority pass with `Blacksmith`: the reforge window is now owner-targeted for remote players, `WndReforge` routes online reforges through a host-owned `blacksmith_reforge` action, and `GameScene` now resolves that action on the host with rejection feedback if the reforge is refused.
- Standardized in-game settings button order to `Back`, `Save`, `Exit to Menu`, so the settings window now follows the same primary/secondary action order the rest of the menu flow uses.
- Tightened the multiplayer feedback path inside `GameScene`: host-authoritative inventory/reward actions now use a hero-targeted message helper for cursed-item removal failures, full-inventory failures, drops, and quest reward receipt, so remote players see those action results on their own client instead of only the host receiving the message.
